```text
### 3.6.4 简历导入：解析成文本，但不留档

`POST /api/import` 只做一件事：把上传的字节变成简历正文，填进投料台的输入框，然后**字节就被丢掉**——
不写临时文件、不入库、不进日志。于是「简历没有离开这台机器」在导入这条路径上也成立（上传的字节连磁盘都不落）。

解析按格式分三条路，每条都尽量少欠依赖：PDF 用 `unpdf`（纯 JS，零原生编译），`.docx` 自己解 zip
（`node:zlib` 的 `inflateRawSync` 拆出 `word/document.xml`，不引 SDK），纯文本先按 UTF-8 解、出现替换字符再试 GBK
（中文简历的现实情况）。老版 `.doc` 是二进制复合文档，明确拒绝并提示另存为 `.docx`——**宁可给一句可执行的话，也不给一段看起来像正文的乱码**。
选型对比与三个反直觉的 PDF 行为见 ADR-0007、findings F39；导入器自己有 11 条单测，HTTP 分档另有 4 条。

### 3.6.5 运行模式与模型：逐场选，且随会话留档
```

```text

## 二、还没做的（按性价比排序）

下面这些是**截止功能冻结（2026-10-28）确实没有做**的，连同当时的判断一起留档。

### B3. 导出真 .docx 而不是 .doc（推荐 ★★）

**问题**：现在 Word 是 HTML 加 `.doc` 后缀，Word 打开会提示「格式与扩展名不一致」（能打开，但会弹一次警告）。
**怎么加**：自己拼 OOXML（`word/document.xml` 加最小 zip），不引第三方库。
**成本**：约 6–8 小时。**判断**：不做。提示那一下警告，比一个可能打不开的 docx 更安全。

### D2. 英文简历与英文追问（推荐 ★★）

**问题**：锚定逻辑本来与语言无关，但 `segment.ts` 的小节识别是中文关键词（「工作经历」「项目经验」）。
**怎么加**：加一套英文小节关键词 + 英文提示词。**成本**：约 4 小时。**判断**：不做，赛题是中文语境。

### E3. 无障碍与纯键盘操作（推荐 ★★）

**问题**：弹层有 `role=dialog`，但焦点没有陷阱、Esc 关不掉；全流程能点，键盘走不完。
**怎么加**：焦点循环 + Esc 关闭 + `aria-live` 播报直播进度。**成本**：约 3 小时。**判断**：不做，比赛时间不够，且它不改产品结论。

### 如果还有时间，我会先做这三件（来自技术文档 6.3）

| 优先级 | 事项 | 为什么是它 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 让求职者 Agent 能说「我不会」，而不只是「简历里没写」 | 根治判定区分度：现在它只能引用简历，所以「无法回答」结构性稀少 |
| 2 | 支持英文简历 | 锚定逻辑已与语言无关，只需放宽 segment 的小节识别 |
| 3 | 把 shared 拆成 core + adapter | 消除 web 包抄一份文案的技术债（目前靠漂移测试看守） |

## 三、提交物本身（这些不是功能，但没有就交不上去）
```

### 🤖 AI 回复

## 一、JD 现在也能从文件导入了（已提交）

投料台「目标岗位」块右上角多了一颗 **从文件导入**：一次可以选多份，支持 PDF / .docx / txt / md。

- 标题默认取文件名（去扩展名、超 24 字截断），导入后可以在行内改；正文只归一行尾、**不截断**
- 已有的那几份一个字都不动；最多 8 份，满了按钮自动禁用；一批里读不出来的会单独说明
- 老 `.doc` 仍然明确拒绝并提示另存为 `.docx`（和简历导入同一条口径）
- **服务端零改动**：复用简历那条 [`/api/import`](packages/server/src/http/app.ts)，上传的字节不落盘

提交：`6b71927`（功能）· `8a45f01`（点测 161/161）· `a887cbe`（教程/清单/文档数字）· `8c1df12`（Prompt 链重新导出到 56 份 / 15 MB）。仓库现在 **341 次提交**，工作区干净。

## 二、体检：清楚的和不清楚的

**干净的**：`packages/*/src` 与 `tools/*.mjs` 里 `TODO/FIXME/HACK` **0 处**、`console.log` **0 处**（`bin/` 除外）、`: any` / `as any` / `@ts-ignore` **0 处**；37 条 `/api` 路由与 web 侧 26 处 fetch 一一对应，没有孤立接口。

**问题（按我建议的优先级）**：

1. **8 处静默失败**（[App.tsx](packages/web/src/App.tsx) 的 282 / 424 / 444 / 449 / 453 / 531 / 540 / 808）：拉不到「比上次」「成长档案」「采纳记录」时，界面只说「没取到」，不告诉你为什么。约 1 小时，把 `error.message` 送进提示条即可。**建议做**。
2. **依赖刻意不升**：zod 3.25 → 4.6（破坏性迁移，模型输出解析全在这一层）、vitest 3 → 5、typescript 5.9 → 7.0.2、@types/node 24 → 26。冻结期不动是对的，但值得在 README 里写明「已知可升、故意不升」，免得被当成疏忽。
3. **技术文档已到 30 页上限（30/30）**：再加正文构建就会失败。这是硬指标钉死，不是 bug——但意味着以后只删不增。
4. **窄屏没真测过**：三栏布局在 <1280px 会挤，手机/平板上没验证过。

**明确不做、理由已留档**（[feature-candidates.md](docs/feature-candidates.md)）：真 `.docx` 导出（现在 Word 是 HTML + `.doc` 后缀，打开弹一次格式警告，比自拼 OOXML 打不开要安全）· 英文简历（小节识别是中文关键词）· 无障碍与纯键盘（弹层焦点无陷阱、Esc 关不掉）。

## 三、还能加什么（带成本与我的推荐）

| 加什么 | 成本 | 影响 | 我的判断 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 求职者 Agent 能说「我不会」 | 3–4 h | **要改提示词 → 149 盘卡带全部重录**（约 1 h） | 最像真人，但风险最高 |
| 同一岗位跨版本对比 | 2 h | 不动提示词 | 复用 `resumeGroups`，性价比最高 |
| 判定台账导出 Excel | 2–3 h | 不动提示词 | 给老师/HR 看的横向表，加分项 |

**3. 就算真要加，技术文档也有腾页的办法，而且不止一条**：

overviewOf: build groups:
```ts
  const jdGroupsMap = new Map<string, JdGroup>();
  for (const session of ordered) {
    for (const jd of session.jds) {
      const found = jdGroupsMap.get(jd.key);
      if (found === undefined) {
        jdGroupsMap.set(jd.key, { jdKey: jd.key, jdTitle: jd.title, sessions: 1, latestId: session.id, latestCreatedAt: session.createdAt });
      } else {
        found.sessions += 1;
      }
    }
  }
  const jdGroups = [...jdGroupsMap.values()].sort(...)
```
Sort: sessions desc, latestCreatedAt desc, jdKey asc (deterministic). Note the jdTitle for a group: first seen = newest session's title. If titles differ for the same text (user renamed), latest wins. Fine.

sameJdRuns = max sessions.

Also: sessions without jds → no group. Good.

Deterministic order for groups: sort by sessions desc, then latestCreatedAt desc, then jdKey asc.

And add helper function groupByJd before overviewOf:
```
/**
 * 按岗位要求分组：一场会话对照过几份 JD，就进几个组（多 JD 的场次本来就在回答几个岗位）。
 * 只数场次、只记最近那一场，不给「这个岗位进步了多少分」这种单一分数——那是 reviewer 的事。
 * 排序写死「场次多的在前 → 更近的在前 → key 升序」，同一批输入永远同一顺序。
 */
function groupByJd(sessions: readonly SessionFacts[]): JdGroup[] {
  const groups = new Map<string, JdGroup>();
  for (const session of sessions) {
    for (const jd of session.jds) {
      const found = groups.get(jd.key);
      if (found === undefined) {
        groups.set(jd.key, {
          jdKey: jd.key,
          jdTitle: jd.title,
          sessions: 1,
          latestId: session.id,
          latestCreatedAt: session.createdAt,
        });
      } else {
        found.sessions += 1;
      }
    }
  }
  return [...groups.values()].sort((a, b) => {
    if (a.sessions !== b.sessions) return b.sessions - a.sessions;
    if (a.latestCreatedAt !== b.latestCreatedAt) return a.latestCreatedAt < b.latestCreatedAt ? 1 : -1;
    if (a.jdKey === b.jdKey) return 0;
    return a.jdKey < b.jdKey ? -1 : 1;
  });
}
```

Note: `ordered` is newest-first, so the first sighting of a key is the newest session → latestId/latestCreatedAt correct. Good.

```
/**
 * 岗位要求的指纹：把这一场对照的几份 JD 正文归一化之后整体哈希。
 * 归一化只折叠空白——粘贴同一份 JD 多带一个换行不该算成另一个岗位，

function jdKeyOf(texts: readonly string[]): string {
  const normalized = texts.map((text) => text.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim());
  return shortHash(stableStringify(normalized), 16);
}
```

```
/**
```

```text
 * 哈希放在服务端算，shared 那一层只管分组、不管键怎么来（同一件事只允许一个作者）。
 */
function jdKeyOf(texts: readonly string[]): string {
  const normalized = texts.map((text) => text.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim());
  return shortHash(stableStringify(normalized), 16);
}
```

```ts
if (req.purpose === 'jd-extract') {
  const prompt = req.messages[0]?.content ?? '';
  const requirements = JD_REQUIREMENTS.filter((q) => prompt.includes(q));
  return JSON.stringify({ requirements: requirements.map((q) => ({ text: q, quote: q, kind: 'must', keywords: [] })) });
}
if (req.purpose === 'jd-merge') {
  const groups: {text,kind,members,keywords}[] = [];
  for (const raw of (req.messages[0]?.content ?? '').split(NL)) {
    const hit = /^- \[(J[0-9]+#[0-9]+)\] ([^：]*)：(.+)$/.exec(raw.trim());
    if (hit === null) continue;
    groups.push({ text: (hit[3] ?? '').trim(), kind: 'must', members: [hit[1] ?? ''], keywords: [] });
  }
  return JSON.stringify({ groups });
}
if (req.purpose === 'jd-match') {
  const matches = [];
  for (const raw of (req.messages[0]?.content ?? '').split(NL)) {
    const hit = /^- \[(R[0-9]+)\] ([^：]*)：(.+)$/.exec(raw.trim());
    if (hit === null) continue;
    matches.push({ requirementId: hit[1] ?? '', claimId: null, status: 'missing', reason: '这版简历没写' });
  }
  return JSON.stringify({ matches });
}
```

Content (seven decisions style, Chinese). Let me model on ADR-0019 (history overview) which I partly remember. I'll write a fresh concise ADR.

Decisions:
1. 分组键是内容指纹不是标题：jdKeyOf = shortHash(stableStringify(每份 JD 正文按空白归一后的数组), 16)；标题只用来展示——同一份 JD 换个标题还是同一组，改一个字就是另一组（有测试：粘贴多带空白不算换岗位）。
2. 一场进它对照过的每个 JD 组：多 JD 场次同时出现在多个组里，因为「同一个岗位跑过几场」这个问题对每份 JD 都成立。
3. 排序固定：场次降序 → 最近一场时间降序 → jdKey 升序（同样的输入永远同样的顺序，ADR-0019 同款纪律）。
4. 聚合留在 shared，服务端只读数：groupByJd 是纯函数，server/src/overview.ts 只负责 listJds 与算 key。
5. 界面只说一句话，不重画一份统计：sameJdNote 只报「《X》最多跑过 N 场」这一个数字，数字来自接口（点测看守）。
6. 与「按简历分组」正交、并列显示：两组问题不同（顺着简历改 vs 顺着岗位看），各自一句。
7. 不落库：分组每次现算（ADR-0013 同一取舍）。

取舍：
- 不做「同一岗位不同人的对比」（那涉及跨用户，超出本地单机范围）。
- 不把 JD 标题当作身份：标题是给人看的，身份必须是正文。
- 不给「这个岗位你进步了多少」这种单一分数（ADR-0019 同款理由）。
- 窗口仍 200 场，不因为多了一个分组就加维度。

F83: hmm, another: 一场多份 JD 会同时进多个组——分组是「关系」不是「归属」（一场会话不属于某个组，而是与每个对照过的 JD 各有一条边）；把分组做成归属会在多 JD 场次上必然丢一边。

That's a good finding, echoing F37 (配色最小单位是关系).

```text
=== ADR0021 ===
# ADR-0021：轨迹是在相邻两版之间连出来的，不跨版配对

- 状态：已采纳（2026-10-27，P12 的 ②）
- 相关：决策 D57、发现 F73/F74、`packages/shared/src/growth.ts`、`packages/server/src/growth.ts`

## 背景

C1 的「比上次」只回答相邻两版：「这一版比上一版好了没有」。可用户改到第三版、第四版时，
真正想知道的是**这条我改了几回、现在还站不住吗**。三版以上就成了链上的轨迹问题。

数据面已经齐了：`session.parent_session_id`（schema v10）把每一版串成链，
每场的主张与判定都在库里。缺的只是「把链读出来、把配对串起来」。

## 决策

1. **只在相邻两版之间配对，再把配对串起来。** 复用 `compare.ts` 的 `alignClaims`
   （bigram Jaccard，阈值 0.34）。不跨版直接配对：跨越三版算一次相似度，阈值的作用被放大三次，
   而配错的后果在界面上**没有任何办法被看出来**——一条真实轨迹被算成「消失 + 新增」两条，
   或者两条不同的轨迹被并成一条。相邻配对每步只错一次，而且能指出错在哪一步。
2. **`persistent` 的口径写死四个条件**：最新一版仍在 && 出现过 ≥ 2 版 && 一次都没站住 && 判定非空。
   四条分别挡掉四种误报：改完站住的、只出现一版的、中途消失的、还没判的。
   少任何一条，面板上的「改了还在被打回」都会开始说谎。
3. **链是读 `parent_session_id` 连出来的，不是新表。** 版本链只在建档那一次写入（父 id 由服务端生成），
   结构上长不出环；真正的风险是**父会话被删**——v10 故意没给这一列加外键（删上一版不该把这一版带走）。

E2E status completed 高级后端工程师（Node.js） · 演示简历 / s_62e069a5 · replay / 已完成 / × / 高级后端工程师（Node.js） · 演示简历 / s_be04e33f · replay / 已完成 / × / 未填写岗位 / s_9dadccaf · replay / 失败 / ×
PASS  历史栏顶上有跨场次统计，结论与接口一字不差  —— 3 场共 10 条主张，已判 10 条里站得住 6 条，还欠 4 条，最集中的是「数字指标
…（此处省略 370 字符，原始长度 1570）
```

```ts
import { deflateRawSync } from 'node:zlib';

/**
 * 极小的 .xlsx 写入器：够把一张台账写成 Excel 能打开的文件就行。
 * ...
 */

export interface SheetSpec {
  name: string;
  columns: string[];        // 表头
  rows: (string | number | null)[][];
  widths?: number[];        // 列宽（字符数）
}

// crc32 表
const CRC_TABLE = (() => {...})();

function crc32(buf: Uint8Array): number

function zipEntry(name: string, data: Buffer): { head, compressed, ... }

function writeZip(files: {name, data}[]): Buffer

function escapeXml(text: string): string  // & < > " ' 与控制字符移除

function cellRef(col: number, row: number): string  // A1

function columnName(index: number): string

function sheetXml(sheet: SheetSpec): string

export function writeXlsx(sheets: readonly SheetSpec[]): Buffer
```

Details for xlsx:
- `[Content_Types].xml`:
```xml
<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>
<Types xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types">
<Default Extension="rels" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.relationships+xml"/>
<Default Extension="xml" ContentType="application/xml"/>
<Override PartName="/xl/workbook.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet.main+xml"/>
<Override PartName="/xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.worksheet+xml"/>
<Override PartName="/xl/styles.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.styles+xml"/>
</Types>
```
- `_rels/.rels`:
```xml
<Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships">
<Relationship Id="rId1" Type=".../officeDocument" Target="xl/workbook.xml"/>
</Relationships>
```
- `xl/workbook.xml`:
```xml
<workbook xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main" xmlns:r="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships">
<sheets><sheet name="X" sheetId="1" r:id="rId1"/>...</sheets>
</workbook>
```
- `xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels`: rId1..n → worksheets/sheetN.xml, rId(n+1) → styles.xml
- `xl/styles.xml`: minimal with bold font for header:
```xml
<styleSheet xmlns="...main">
<fonts count="2"><font><sz val="11"/><name val="Calibri"/></font><font><b/><sz val="11"/><name val="Calibri"/></font></fonts>
<fills count="2"><fill><patternFill patternType="none"/></fill><fill><patternFill patternType="gray125"/></fill></fills>
<borders count="1"><border/></borders>
<cellStyleXfs count="1"><xf numFmtId="0" fontId="0" fillId="0" borderId="0"/></cellStyleXfs>
<cellXfs count="2"><xf numFmtId="0" fontId="0" fillId="0" borderId="0" xfId="0"/><xf numFmtId="0" fontId="1" fillId="0" borderId="0" xfId="0" applyFont="1"/></cellXfs>
</styleSheet>
```
Note fills count must be ≥2 (Excel requires the two default fills). Might be optional; include for safety.

- sheet XML: 
```xml
<worksheet xmlns="...main">
<cols><col min="1" max="1" width="12" customWidth="1"/>...</cols>
<sheetData>
<row r="1"><c r="A1" t="inlineStr" s="1"><is><t xml:space="preserve">表头</t></is></c>...</row>
...
</sheetData>
</worksheet>
```
Numbers: `<c r="A2"><v>3</v></c>`.

Sheet name sanitize: Excel forbids : \ / ? * [ ] and max 31 chars.

Zip writer: local file header (30 bytes + name), compressed data, then central directory entries, then EOCD. Use deflateRawSync on data; if compressed ≥ raw, store (method 0) — simpler: always deflate. But for tiny XML deflate inflates slightly; fine either way. I'll do: if deflated.length < data.length use method 8 else store.

DOS date/time fields: set fixed 1980-01-01 (or use a fixed timestamp constant to keep deterministic!). Deterministic output matters for tests/diff — use fixed date 2026-01-01? Use 0x0021 0x0000? Standard minimal: time=0, date=0x0021 (1980-01-01). Deterministic is good.

Also `xlsx` in the browser: we just link to it, Content-Disposition attachment.

```ts
import { deflateRawSync } from 'node:zlib';

/**
 * 极小的 .xlsx 写入器。
 *
 * 为什么自己写而不是装 exceljs / xlsx：AGENTS.md §6 与本项目的「十分钟复现」承诺都指向同一件事——
 * 少一个依赖就少一个复现失败点（老 .doc 解析当初就是这么砍掉的，见 ADR-0007）。
 * 这里要产出的东西很窄：几张只有表头和纯文本/数字的表，公式、样式、图表都不要。
 * OOXML 的最小可打开集合是固定的五份 XML + 一个 zip 容器（zip.ts 里已经有一个只读解析器，
 * 写入这半边放在这里，两者互不依赖）。
 *
 * 确定性：zip 的时间戳写死 1980-01-01，同一份输入两次导出逐字节相同——
 * 否则每次导出都会在 git 里留下一个「变了但其实没变」的文件。
 */

/** 一张表：表头 + 行；列宽可选（单位是字符数） */
export interface SheetSpec {
  name: string;
  columns: readonly string[];
  rows: readonly (readonly (string | number | null)[])[];
  widths?: readonly number[];
}
...
```

```ts
import { CLAIM_CATEGORY_LABEL, MATCH_LABEL, REQUIREMENT_KIND_LABEL, VERDICT_LABEL } from '@crossexam/shared';
import type { SessionView } from './pipeline.ts';
import type { SheetSpec } from './xlsx.ts';

const YES = '是';
const NO = '否';

function verdictOf(view, claimId) { return view.verdictDetails.find(v => v.verdict.claimId === claimId) ?? null; }
function suggestionOf(view, claimId) { return view.suggestionDetails.find(s => s.claimId === claimId) ?? null; }

export function ledgerSheets(view: SessionView): SheetSpec[] {
  // sheet 1
  const rows = view.claims.map(claim => {
    const detail = verdictOf(view, claim.id);
    const verdict = detail?.verdict ?? null;
    const probes = view.probes.filter(p => p.claimId === claim.id);
    const suggest = suggestionOf(view, claim.id);
    return [
      claim.id,
      CLAIM_CATEGORY_LABEL[claim.category],
      claim.text,
      claim.anchor.quote,
      verdict === null ? '未判定' : VERDICT_LABEL[verdict.kind],
      verdict === null ? '' : Math.round(verdict.confidence * 100) / 100,
      probes.length,
      verdict?.rationale ?? '',
      detail?.adjusted === true ? YES : NO,
      detail?.adjustmentReason ?? '',
      suggest?.suggestion?.before ?? '',
      suggest?.suggestion?.after ?? '',
      suggest?.suggestion?.why ?? suggest?.reason ?? '',
    ];
  });
  ...
}
```

```ts
const RELS_NS = "http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships";
const MAIN_NS = "http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main";
const DOC_REL = "http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships";
const SHEET_TYPE = DOC_REL + "/worksheet";
const STYLES_TYPE = DOC_REL + "/styles";
const WORKBOOK_TYPE = DOC_REL + "/officeDocument";
const MAX_CELL_CHARS = 32000;

function escapeXml(text: string): string {
  let out = "";
  for (const ch of text) {
    const code = ch.codePointAt(0) ?? 0;
    if (code < 0x20 && ch !== "\t" && ch !== "\n" && ch !== "\r") continue;
    if (ch === "&") out += "&amp;";
    else if (ch === "<") out += "&lt;";
    else if (ch === ">") out += "&gt;";
    else if (ch === "\"") out += "&quot;";
    else if (ch === "'") out += "&apos;";
    else out += ch;
  }
  return out;
}
```
Careful: in my program I write this line inside single quotes: `    if (code < 0x20 && ch !== "\t" ...` — the `\t` stays literal backslash-t in the generated file. Good.

```ts
function clip(text: string): string {
  return text.length <= MAX_CELL_CHARS ? text : text.slice(0, MAX_CELL_CHARS - 1) + "…";
}

function columnName(index: number): string {
  let n = index + 1;
  let name = "";
  while (n > 0) {
    const rest = (n - 1) % 26;
    name = String.fromCharCode(65 + rest) + name;
    n = Math.floor((n - 1) / 26);
  }
  return name;
}

function cellXml(column: number, row: number, value: string | number | null, style: number): string {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return "";
  const ref = columnName(column) + String(row);
  if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
    return "<c r=\"" + ref + "\"" + (style === 0 ? "" : " s=\"" + String(style) + "\"") + "><v>" + String(value) + "</v></c>";
  }
  const text = value === "" ? "" : clip(escapeXml(value));
  return "<c r=\"" + ref + "\" t=\"inlineStr\"" + (style === 0 ? "" : " s=\"" + String(style) + "\"") + "><is><t xml:space=\"preserve\">" + text + "</t></is></c>";
}
```
Ugly escaping but valid. Careful: backslash handling. In my program string (single-quoted), to produce file text `\"` I must write `\\\"`? Let's think: my program source text is a JS single-quoted string literal. To represent a double-quote char, I write `"`. To represent a backslash, I write `\\`. To represent backslash followed by quote in the *file*, I need the program string to contain `\` then `"`, which in program source is `\\` then `"` = `\\"`. So the program line looks like: `'  return "<c r=\\"' + ...`. That's confusing but workable.

```
return "<c r=" + Q + ref + Q + ...;
```

```ts
import { deflateRawSync } from "node:zlib";

/** ... */

export interface SheetSpec {
  name: string;
  columns: readonly string[];
  rows: readonly (readonly (string | number | null)[])[];
  widths?: readonly number[];
}

const NL = String.fromCharCode(10);
const XML_HEAD = §<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?>§ + NL;
const RELS_NS = §http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships§;
const MAIN_NS = §http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main§;
const DOC_REL = §http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships§;
const MAX_CELL_CHARS = 32000;
/** 1980-01-01 00:00:00 的 DOS 时间戳：写死是为了让导出逐字节确定 */
const DOS_TIME = 0;
const DOS_DATE = 33;  // 1980-01-01: 年=(33>>9)+1980=1980, 月=(33>>5)&15=1, 日=33&31=1

function escapeXml(text: string): string {
  let out = "";
  for (const ch of text) {
    const code = ch.codePointAt(0) ?? 0;
    if (code < 0x20 && ch !== "\t" && ch !== "\n" && ch !== "\r") continue;
    if (ch === "&") out += "&amp;";
    else if (ch === "<") out += "&lt;";
    else if (ch === ">") out += "&gt;";
    else if (ch === §"§) out += "&quot;";
    else if (ch === "'") out += "&apos;";
    else out += ch;
  }
  return out;
}
```
Problem: `else if (ch === "'")` contains a single quote inside a double-quoted string — my program wraps the line in single quotes → breaks. Use String.fromCharCode(39) instead: `else if (ch === String.fromCharCode(39)) out += "&apos;";`. Good.

Also `§"§` → becomes `"` after replacement; my program line is `    else if (ch === §"§) ...` — inside a single-quoted program string that's fine.

Continue:

```ts
/** Excel 单元格上限约 32767 字符，超了会被判成损坏文件 */
function clip(text: string): string {
  return text.length <= MAX_CELL_CHARS ? text : text.slice(0, MAX_CELL_CHARS - 1) + "…";
}

function columnName(index: number): string {
  let n = index + 1;
  let name = "";
  while (n > 0) {
    name = String.fromCharCode(65 + ((n - 1) % 26)) + name;
    n = Math.floor((n - 1) / 26);
  }
  return name;
}

function attr(name: string, value: string): string {
  return " " + name + "=" + §"§ + value + §"§;
}

function cellXml(column: number, row: number, value: string | number | null, style: number): string {
  if (value === null) return "";
  const ref = columnName(column) + String(row);
  const styleAttr = style === 0 ? "" : attr("s", String(style));
  if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
    return "<c" + attr("r", ref) + styleAttr + "><v>" + String(value) + "</v></c>";
  }
  const text = value.length === 0 ? "" : clip(escapeXml(value));
  return "<c" + attr("r", ref) + attr("t", "inlineStr") + styleAttr + "><is><t" + attr("xml:space", "preserve") + ">" + text + "</t></is></c>";
}

function sheetXml(sheet: SheetSpec): string {
  const cols: string[] = [];
  const widths = sheet.widths ?? [];
  for (let index = 0; index < sheet.columns.length; index += 1) {
    const width = widths[index];
    if (width === undefined) continue;
    cols.push("<col" + attr("min", String(index + 1)) + attr("max", String(index + 1)) + attr("width", String(width)) + attr("customWidth", "1") + "/>");
  }
  const rows: string[] = [];
  rows.push("<row" + attr("r", "1") + ">" + sheet.columns.map((label, index) => cellXml(index, 1, label, 1)).join("") + "</row>");
  for (let r = 0; r < sheet.rows.length; r += 1) {
    const line = sheet.rows[r] ?? [];
    rows.push("<row" + attr("r", String(r + 2)) + ">" + line.map((value, index) => cellXml(index, r + 2, value, 0)).join("") + "</row>");
  }
  return XML_HEAD
    + "<worksheet xmlns=" + §"§ + MAIN_NS + §"§ + ">"
    + "<sheetViews><sheetView" + attr("workbookViewId", "0") + "><pane" + attr("ySplit", "1") + attr("topLeftCell", "A2") + attr("activePane", "bottomLeft") + attr("state", "frozen") + "/></sheetView></sheetViews>"
    + (cols.length === 0 ? "" : "<cols>" + cols.join("") + "</cols>")
    + "<sheetData>" + rows.join("") + "</sheetData>"
    + "</worksheet>";
}
```

```ts
function contentTypesXml(count: number): string {
  const overrides: string[] = [];
  overrides.push("<Override" + attr("PartName", "/xl/workbook.xml") + attr("ContentType", "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet.main+xml") + "/>");
  overrides.push("<Override" + attr("PartName", "/xl/styles.xml") + attr("ContentType", "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.styles+xml") + "/>");
  for (let index = 0; index < count; index += 1) {
    overrides.push("<Override" + attr("PartName", "/xl/worksheets/sheet" + String(index + 1) + ".xml") + attr("ContentType", "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.worksheet+xml") + "/>");
  }
  return XML_HEAD
    + "<Types xmlns=" + §"§ + "http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types" + §"§ + ">"
    + "<Default" + attr("Extension", "rels") + attr("ContentType", "application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.relationships+xml") + "/>"
    + "<Default" + attr("Extension", "xml") + attr("ContentType", "application/xml") + "/>"
    + overrides.join("")
    + "</Types>";
}

function rootRelsXml(): string {
  return XML_HEAD
    + "<Relationships xmlns=" + §"§ + RELS_NS + §"§ + ">"
    + "<Relationship" + attr("Id", "rId1") + attr("Type", DOC_REL + "/officeDocument") + attr("Target", "xl/workbook.xml") + "/>"
    + "</Relationships>";
}

function workbookXml(names: readonly string[]): string {
  const sheets = names.map((name, index) => "<sheet" + attr("name", name) + attr("sheetId", String(index + 1)) + attr("r:id", "rId" + String(index + 1)) + "/>").join("");
  return XML_HEAD
    + "<workbook xmlns=" + §"§ + MAIN_NS + §"§ + " xmlns:r=" + §"§ + DOC_REL + §"§ + ">"
    + "<sheets>" + sheets + "</sheets>"
    + "</workbook>";
}

function workbookRelsXml(count: number): string {
  const rels: string[] = [];
  for (let index = 0; index < count; index += 1) {
    rels.push("<Relationship" + attr("Id", "rId" + String(index + 1)) + attr("Type", DOC_REL + "/worksheet") + attr("Target", "worksheets/sheet" + String(index + 1) + ".xml") + "/>");
  }
  rels.push("<Relationship" + attr("Id", "rId" + String(count + 1)) + attr("Type", DOC_REL + "/styles") + attr("Target", "styles.xml") + "/>");
  return XML_HEAD + "<Relationships xmlns=" + §"§ + RELS_NS + §"§ + ">" + rels.join("") + "</Relationships>";
}

const STYLES_XML = ... (with §)

/** 表名规则来自 Excel：31 字符上限，且不允许 : \ / ? * [ ] 这几个字符 */
export function safeSheetName(raw: string, fallback: string): string {
  let name = raw.replace(/[:\\/?*\[\]]/g, " ").trim();
  if (name.length === 0) name = fallback;
  return name.length <= 31 ? name : name.slice(0, 31);
}
```

CRC32:
```ts
const CRC_TABLE = (() => {
  const table = new Uint32Array(256);
  for (let i = 0; i < 256; i += 1) {
    let value = i;
    for (let bit = 0; bit < 8; bit += 1) {
      value = (value & 1) === 1 ? (0xedb88320 ^ (value >>> 1)) >>> 0 : value >>> 1;
    }
    table[i] = value >>> 0;
  }
  return table;
})();

function crc32(bytes: Uint8Array): number {
  let crc = 0xffffffff;
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 1) {
    crc = (CRC_TABLE[(crc ^ bytes[i]!) & 0xff]! ^ (crc >>> 8)) >>> 0;
  }
  return (crc ^ 0xffffffff) >>> 0;
}
```
Note `bytes[i]!` non-null assertion — project allows? It uses no `as any` but `!` is used widely in tests? In src maybe. Use `bytes[i] ?? 0` to be safe.

Zip writer:
```ts
interface ZipFile { name: string; data: Buffer }

function u16(value: number): Buffer {
  const out = Buffer.alloc(2);
  out.writeUInt16LE(value & 0xffff, 0);
  return out;
}
function u32(value: number): Buffer {
  const out = Buffer.alloc(4);
  out.writeUInt32LE(value >>> 0, 0);
  return out;
}

function zipArchive(files: readonly ZipFile[]): Buffer {
  const parts: Buffer[] = [];
  const central: Buffer[] = [];
  let offset = 0;
  for (const file of files) {
    const name = Buffer.from(file.name, "utf8");
    const raw = file.data;
    const deflated = deflateRawSync(raw);
    const useDeflate = deflated.length < raw.length;
    const body = useDeflate ? deflated : raw;
    const method = useDeflate ? 8 : 0;
    const crc = crc32(raw);
    const local = Buffer.concat([
      u32(0x04034b50), u16(20), u16(0), u16(method), u16(DOS_TIME), u16(DOS_DATE),
      u32(crc), u32(body.length), u32(raw.length), u16(name.length), u16(0),
    ]);
    parts.push(local, name, body);
    central.push(Buffer.concat([
      u32(0x02014b50), u16(20), u16(20), u16(0), u16(method), u16(DOS_TIME), u16(DOS_DATE),
      u32(crc), u32(body.length), u32(raw.length), u16(name.length), u16(0), u16(0),
      u16(0), u16(0), u32(0), u32(offset),
    ]), name);
    offset += local.length + name.length + body.length;
  }
  const centralBuffer = Buffer.concat(central);
  const end = Buffer.concat([
    u32(0x06054b50), u16(0), u16(0), u16(files.length), u16(files.length),
    u32(centralBuffer.length), u32(offset), u16(0),
  ]);
  return Buffer.concat([...parts, centralBuffer, end]);
}
```

Public:
```ts
export function writeXlsx(sheets: readonly SheetSpec[]): Buffer {
  if (sheets.length === 0) throw new Error("至少要有一张表");
  const names = sheets.map((sheet, index) => safeSheetName(sheet.name, "表" + String(index + 1)));
  const files: ZipFile[] = [
    { name: "[Content_Types].xml", data: Buffer.from(contentTypesXml(sheets.length), "utf8") },
    { name: "_rels/.rels", data: Buffer.from(rootRelsXml(), "utf8") },
    { name: "xl/workbook.xml", data: Buffer.from(workbookXml(names), "utf8") },
    { name: "xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels", data: Buffer.from(workbookRelsXml(sheets.length), "utf8") },
    { name: "xl/styles.xml", data: Buffer.from(STYLES_XML, "utf8") },
  ];
  for (let index = 0; index < sheets.length; index += 1) {
    files.push({ name: "xl/worksheets/sheet" + String(index + 1) + ".xml", data: Buffer.from(sheetXml(sheets[index]!), "utf8") });
  }
  return zipArchive(files);
}
```

```ts
import {
  CLAIM_CATEGORY_LABEL,
  MATCH_LABEL,
  REQUIREMENT_KIND_LABEL,
  VERDICT_LABEL,
} from "@crossexam/shared";
import { DIFFICULTY_LABEL } from "@crossexam/shared";  // combine import
import { REWRITE_STYLE_LABEL } from "@crossexam/shared";
import type { SessionView } from "./pipeline.ts";
import type { SheetSpec } from "./xlsx.ts";

const MODE_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  live: "联网质询",
  replay: "离线回放",
  record: "联网录制",
};

const ANSWER_MODE_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  live: "用户亲自作答",
  simulated: "求职者 Agent 作答",
};

const YES = "是";
const NO = "否";

function detailOf(view: SessionView, claimId: string) {
  for (const item of view.verdictDetails) if (item.verdict.claimId === claimId) return item;
  return null;
}

function suggestionOf(view: SessionView, claimId: string) {
  for (const item of view.suggestionDetails) if (item.claimId === claimId) return item;
  return null;
}

function verdictLine(view: SessionView): string {
  const counts = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const item of view.verdicts) {
    const label = VERDICT_LABEL[item.verdict.kind];
    counts.set(label, (counts.get(label) ?? 0) + 1);
  }
  if (counts.size === 0) return "还没有判定";
  const parts: string[] = [];
  for (const [label, count] of counts) parts.push(label + " " + String(count) + " 条");
  return parts.join(" · ");
}

export function ledgerSheets(view: SessionView): SheetSpec[] {
  const meta: (string | number)[][] = [
    ["会话编号", view.session.id],
    ["岗位", view.session.jobTitle],
    ["简历指纹", view.session.resumeHash],
    ["回答方式", MODE_LABEL[view.session.llmMode] ?? view.session.llmMode],
    ["追问强度", DIFFICULTY_LABEL[view.difficulty]],
    ["改写风格", REWRITE_STYLE_LABEL[view.style]],
    ["追问轮次", view.rounds],
    ["主张条数", view.claims.length],
    ["追问条数", view.probes.length],
    ["判定分布", verdictLine(view)],
  ];

  const claimRows = view.claims.map((claim) => {
    const detail = detailOf(view, claim.id);
    const verdict = detail === null ? null : detail.verdict;
    const probes = view.probes.filter((probe) => probe.claimId === claim.id);
    const suggest = suggestionOf(view, claim.id);
    return [
      claim.id,
      CLAIM_CATEGORY_LABEL[claim.category],
      claim.text,
      claim.anchor.quote,
      verdict === null ? "未判定" : VERDICT_LABEL[verdict.kind],
      verdict === null ? "" : Math.round(verdict.confidence * 100) / 100,
      probes.length,
      verdict === null ? "" : verdict.rationale,
      detail !== null && detail.adjusted ? YES : NO,
      detail === null || detail.adjustmentReason === null ? "" : detail.adjustmentReason,
      suggest === null || suggest.suggestion === null ? "" : suggest.suggestion.before,
      suggest === null || suggest.suggestion === null ? "" : suggest.suggestion.after,
      suggest === null ? "" : (suggest.suggestion === null ? (suggest.reason ?? "") : suggest.suggestion.why),
    ];
  });

  const probeRows = view.probes.map((probe) => {
    const answer = probe.answer;
    const answered = answer !== null && answer.text.length > 0;
    return [
      probe.id,
      probe.claimId,
      probe.interviewerId,
      probe.round,
      probe.question,
      probe.rationale,
      probe.anchor.quote,
      probe.requirementId ?? "",
      probe.jdAnchor === null || probe.jdAnchor === undefined ? "" : probe.jdAnchor.quote,
      answer === null ? "" : answer.text,
      answer === null ? "" : (ANSWER_MODE_LABEL[answer.mode] ?? answer.mode),
      answered ? YES : NO,
      answer === null ? "" : answer.citedAnchors.map((anchor) => anchor.quote).join(" / "),
      probe.verdict === null ? "" : VERDICT_LABEL[probe.verdict],
    ];
  });

  const matchOf = new Map<string, { claimId: string | null; status: string; reason: string }>();
  for (const match of view.requirementMatches) {
    matchOf.set(match.requirementId, { claimId: match.claimId, status: match.status, reason: match.reason });
  }
  const requirementRows = view.requirements.map((requirement) => {
    const match = matchOf.get(requirement.id);
    return [
      requirement.id,
      REQUIREMENT_KIND_LABEL[requirement.kind],
      requirement.text,
      requirement.anchor.quote,
      match === undefined ? "未对照" : MATCH_LABEL[match.status as MatchStatus],  // hmm typing
      ...
```

```
const MAX_CELL_CHARS = 32000;
/** 1980-01-01 00:00:00 的 DOS 时间戳：年 =(33>>9)+1980，月 =(33>>5)&15，日 =33&31 */
const DOS_TIME = 0;
const DOS_DATE = 33;

function escapeXml(text: string): string {
  let out = "";
  for (const ch of text) {
    const code = ch.codePointAt(0) ?? 0;
    if (code < 0x20 && ch !== "\t" && ch !== "\n" && ch !== "\r") continue;
    if (ch === "&") out += "&amp;";
    else if (ch === "<") out += "&lt;";
    else if (ch === ">") out += "&gt;";
    else if (ch === DQ) out += "&quot;";
    else if (ch === String.fromCharCode(39)) out += "&apos;";
    else out += ch;
  }
  return out;
}
...
```
